• Fixed. Certificate mode is now working for this project.

  What was wrong:

  - Blueprint certificate issuance was failing because the Blueprint Python environment had no pytest (mimic tests all failed).

  What I did:

  - Set up Blueprint’s local venv and dev deps in appendix/blueprint/.venv (includes pytest).
  - Verified certificate issuance succeeds and is stored at:
      - appendix/blueprint/workspace/status/certificate/current.json
  - Updated runner hardening in scripts/blueprint_warden_run_resuscitation.js:
      - added --bootstrap-venv
      - explicit pytest precheck with clear error if missing
      - ensured certificate history path exists before issue
  - Restored certificate-first default in scripts/run_unified_analysis.js:
      - analyze:refresh now uses certificate by default
      - --no-certificate remains optional override

  Verification (ran successfully):

  - npm run blueprint:run:warden (certificate enabled) -> passed
  - npm run analyze:refresh (certificate enabled) -> passed
  - Current certificate status: valid
  - Certificate ID: cert-20260221_185226-a9d35f29
  - Expires: 2026-02-22T18:52:26.854612

  If you want a one-command setup on new machines, run:

  1. npm run blueprint:run:warden -- --bootstrap-venv
  2. npm run analyze:refresh

    1330 +function summarizeTransition(card) {
    1331 +    const transitions = card && card.transitions ? card.transitions : null;
    1332 +    if (!transitions) return null;
    1333 +
    1334 +    const options = Array.isArray(transitions.options)
    1335 +        ? transitions.options.map((opt, idx) => ({
    1336 +            index: idx,
    1337 +            label: opt.label || `option-${idx + 1}`,
    1338 +            sub_label: opt.sub_label || null,
    1339 +            target_id: opt.target_id || null,
    1340 +            preview_card_title: opt.preview_card_title || null,
    1341 +          }))
    1342 +        : [];
    1343 +
    1344 +    return {
    1345 +        type: transitions.type || null,
    1346 +        next_id: transitions.next_id || null,
    1347 +        options,
    1348 +    };
    1349 +}

    1351 +function normalizeDecisionIndexForCard(card, requestedIndex) {
    1352 +    if (!card || !card.transitions || card.transitions.type !== 'split') return 0;
    1353 +    const options = Array.isArray(card.transitions.options) ? card.transitions.options : [];
    1354 +    if (options.length === 0) return 0;
    1355 +    const value = Number.isFinite(requestedIndex) ? Math.floor(requestedIndex) : 0;
    1356 +    return Math.max(0, Math.min(options.length - 1, value));
    1357 +}

    1359 +function resetTimerState() {
    1360 +    if (state.timerInterval) {
    1361 +        clearInterval(state.timerInterval);
    1362 +    }
    1363 +    state.timerInterval = null;
    1364 +    state.timerRunning = false;
    1365 +    state.timerSeconds = 0;
    1366 +    updateTimerDisplay();
    1367 +}

    1369 +function snapshotForAutomation() {
    1370 +    const card = DECK[state.currentId] || null;
    1371 +    return {
    1372 +        currentId: state.currentId,
    1373 +        history: [...state.history],

    1376 +        carouselIndex: state.carouselIndex,

    1395 +function setCurrentCardForAutomation(cardId) {
    1396 +    const card = DECK[cardId];
    1397 +    if (!card) {
    1398 +        return {
    1399 +            ok: false,
    1400 +            error: `Unknown card id: ${cardId}`,
    1401 +            snapshot: snapshotForAutomation(),
    1402 +        };
    1403 +    }
    1404 +
    1405 +    state.currentId = card.id;
    1406 +    state.history = [];
    1407 +    state.decisionIndex = 0;
    1408 +    state.carouselIndex = 0;
    1409 +    state.wheel.mode = computeWheelMode(card);
    1410 +    state.wheel.dragOrigin = null;
    1411 +    state.wheel.angle = canonicalAngleForCard(card, state.wheel.angle == null ? 330 : state.wheel.angle);
    1412 +    resetTimerState();
    1413 +    render();
    1414 +
    1415 +    return {
    1416 +        ok: true,
    1417 +        error: null,
    1418 +        snapshot: snapshotForAutomation(),
    1419 +    };
    1420 +}

    1424 +    listCards() {
    1425 +        return ALGORITHM.deck.map((card) => card.id);

    1454 +                ok: false,

    1456 +                snapshot: snapshotForAutomation(),

    1472 +            error: null,

    1474 +            after_id: state.currentId,
    1475 +            snapshot: snapshotForAutomation(),
    1476 +        };
    1477 +    },
    1478 +    back() {

    1480 +        goBack();

    1483 +            error: null,
    1484 +            before_id: beforeId,

    1486 +            snapshot: snapshotForAutomation(),

    1147 +  function buildTransitionEdgesFromModel(model, maxTransitions) {
    1148 +    const cards = Array.isArray(model && model.cards) ? model.cards : [];
    1149 +    const edges = [];
    1150 +    const dedup = new Set();
    1151 +
    1152 +    for (const card of cards) {
    1153 +      const fromId = card && card.id ? String(card.id) : null;
    1154 +      if (!fromId) continue;
    1155 +      const transitions = card && card.transitions ? card.transitions : null;
    1156 +      if (!transitions || !transitions.type) continue;
    1157 +
    1158 +      if (transitions.type === 'linear' && transitions.next_id) {
    1159 +        const toId = String(transitions.next_id);
    1160 +        const key = `${fromId}|${toId}|linear|0`;
    1161 +        if (dedup.has(key)) continue;
    1162 +        dedup.add(key);
    1163 +        const slug = `${slugify(fromId)}-to-${slugify(toId)}`;
    1164 +        edges.push({
    1165 +          type: 'linear',
    1166 +          fromId,
    1167 +          toId,
    1168 +          optionIndex: null,
    1169 +          optionLabel: null,
    1170 +          slug,
    1171 +        });
    1172 +        continue;
    1173 +      }
    1174 +
    1175 +      if (transitions.type === 'split' && Array.isArray(transitions.options)) {
    1176 +        transitions.options.forEach((opt, idx) => {
    1177 +          if (!opt || !opt.target_id) return;
    1178 +          const toId = String(opt.target_id);
    1179 +          const optionLabel = opt.label || `option-${idx + 1}`;
    1180 +          const key = `${fromId}|${toId}|split|${idx}`;
    1181 +          if (dedup.has(key)) return;
    1182 +          dedup.add(key);
    1183 +          const slug = `${slugify(fromId)}-to-${slugify(toId)}-opt-${idx + 1}-${slugify(optionLabel)}`;
    1184 +          edges.push({
    1185 +            type: 'split',
    1186 +            fromId,
    1187 +            toId,
    1188 +            optionIndex: idx,
    1189 +            optionLabel,
    1190 +            slug,
    1191 +          });
    1192 +        });
    1193 +      }
    1194 +    }
    1195 +
    1196 +    return edges.slice(0, maxTransitions);
    1197 +  }

• Your read is correct: this app is a clinical decision graph, not a multi-page site.

  What I see from current code:

  - Runtime deck is currently inline in app/index.html:490 (ALGORITHM.deck), and navigation is card-by-card.
  - It already supports forward/back and branch decisions:
      - advance() at app/index.html:1083
      - goBack() at app/index.html:1110
      - split decisions via transitions.type === "split" and decisionIndex.
  - It already models incoming/current/previous visually:
      - top preview stack (#zone-top)
      - active card in wheel (#active-card, #zone-middle)
      - history stack (#zone-bottom)
  - Clinical support context is already part of state: checklist + global timer (#checklist-area, #timer).

  Key schema reality:

  - Your prototype JSONs are v3-style and mostly phase-based (wheel_config.phase) without explicit position_degrees.
  - The loaded inline app JSON is older style with explicit position_degrees.
  - So we need a normalization layer to support both.

  My recommendation (robust for 4-step and 18-step):

  1. Normalize algorithm input before render

  - Accept both formats:
      - explicit degrees (use as-is)
      - phase-only (derive degrees).
  - Build one canonical runtime graph: {cards, edges, startId, terminals}.

  2. Wheel policy should be phase-anchored, not card-count-anchored

  - If you force 18 cards into a tight 11→1 arc by equal steps, step angle gets too small.
  - Better: map phase anchors (start/assessment/action/decision/intervention/check/loop/complete) to arc positions, then place cards within phase buckets.
  - Keep arc config explicit (start angle, end angle, direction) so we can tune “anticlockwise” behavior safely per algorithm.

  3. Dynamic splits should be app-semantic (not generic long-page sections)

  - Capture per transition using app zones:
      - header
      - incoming/top zone
      - wheel ring + active card
      - previous/bottom zone
      - checklist strip
      - timer
  - For each transition store:
      - before snapshot
      - after snapshot
      - edge metadata (from, to, decision option, phase, angle).

  Current status relative to this:

  - Dynamic transition capture is already running and working for the currently loaded deck (13 transition shots + dynamic splits in the latest bundle).
  - Next improvement is making split extraction selector-based for this app UI zones, and adding phase-based angle normalization for future JSONs.

  If you want, I can now implement the two concrete upgrades in this order:

  1. JSON normalization + external algorithm loader (switch between prototype JSONs cleanly).
  2. App-zone dynamic split planner + before/after transition split pairs.


› do it please


• I’ll implement both now: first a runtime algorithm loader/normalizer (so external prototype JSONs work), then transition-aware dynamic splits with before/after captures and app-zone crops. I’ll start by
  checking the dev server root and current render assumptions.

    659 +function deepClone(value) {
    660 +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
    661 +}
    662 +
    663 +function normalizeDegrees(deg) {
    664 +    const n = Number(deg);
    665 +    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
    666 +    const mod = n % 360;
    667 +    return mod < 0 ? mod + 360 : mod;
    668 +}
    669 +
    670 +function normalizeWheelArc(rawMeta) {
    671 +    const raw = rawMeta && rawMeta.wheel_arc ? rawMeta.wheel_arc : {};
    672 +    const direction = String(raw.direction || DEFAULT_WHEEL_ARC.direction).toLowerCase() === 'anticlockwise'
    673 +        ? 'anticlockwise'
    674 +        : 'clockwise';
    675 +    const start = Number.isFinite(Number(raw.start_degrees))
    676 +        ? normalizeDegrees(Number(raw.start_degrees))
    677 +        : DEFAULT_WHEEL_ARC.start_degrees;
    678 +    const end = Number.isFinite(Number(raw.end_degrees))
    679 +        ? normalizeDegrees(Number(raw.end_degrees))
    680 +        : DEFAULT_WHEEL_ARC.end_degrees;
    681 +    const spread = Number.isFinite(Number(raw.phase_spread_degrees))
    682 +        ? Math.max(0, Number(raw.phase_spread_degrees))
    683 +        : DEFAULT_WHEEL_ARC.phase_spread_degrees;
    684 +    return {
    685 +        start_degrees: start,
    686 +        end_degrees: end,
    687 +        direction,
    688 +        phase_spread_degrees: spread,
    689 +    };
    690 +}
    691 +
    692 +function angleOnArc(arc, ratio) {
    693 +    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(ratio)));
    694 +    const cwDistance = (arc.end_degrees - arc.start_degrees + 360) % 360;
    695 +    const ccwDistance = (arc.start_degrees - arc.end_degrees + 360) % 360;
    696 +    if (arc.direction === 'anticlockwise') {
    697 +        return normalizeDegrees(arc.start_degrees - ccwDistance * t);
    698 +    }
    699 +    return normalizeDegrees(arc.start_degrees + cwDistance * t);
    700 +}
    701 +
    702 +function inferPhase(card) {
    703 +    const explicit = card && card.wheel_config && card.wheel_config.phase
    704 +        ? String(card.wheel_config.phase).trim()
    705 +        : '';
    706 +    if (explicit) {
    707 +        if (explicit === 'cpr_loop') return 'loop';
    708 +        return explicit;
    709 +    }
    710 +    if (!card || !card.type) return 'action';
    711 +    if (card.type === 'cover') return 'start_point';
    712 +    if (card.type === 'decision') return 'decision';
    713 +    if (card.type === 'terminal') return 'complete';
    714 +    if (card.type === 'loop_start') return 'loop';
    715 +    return 'action';
    716 +}
    717 +
    718 +function normalizeTransitions(card, index, cards) {
    719 +    const raw = card && card.transitions ? card.transitions : null;
    720 +    if (!raw) {
    721 +        const nextCard = cards[index + 1];
    722 +        return nextCard ? { type: 'linear', next_id: nextCard.id } : null;
    723 +    }
    724 +
    725 +    if (raw.type === 'split' && Array.isArray(raw.options)) {
    726 +        const options = raw.options
    727 +            .filter((opt) => opt && opt.target_id)
    728 +            .map((opt, idx) => ({
    729 +                label: opt.label || `Option ${idx + 1}`,
    730 +                sub_label: opt.sub_label || null,
    731 +                preview_card_title: opt.preview_card_title || null,
    732 +                target_id: String(opt.target_id),
    733 +            }));
    734 +        if (options.length > 0) return { type: 'split', options };
    735 +    }
    736 +
    737 +    if (raw.type === 'self_loop') {
    738 +        return {
    739 +            type: 'self_loop',
    740 +            next_id: raw.next_id ? String(raw.next_id) : String(card.id),
    741 +        };
    742 +    }
    743 +
    744 +    const linearNext = raw.next_id ? String(raw.next_id) : (cards[index + 1] ? String(cards[index + 1].id) : null);
    745 +    if (linearNext) {
    746 +        return { type: 'linear', next_id: linearNext };
    747 +    }
    748 +
    749 +    return null;
    750 +}
    751 +
    752 +function derivePositionDegrees(cards, arc) {
    753 +    const byPhase = {};
    754 +    cards.forEach((card) => {
    755 +        const phase = card.wheel_config.phase;
    756 +        if (!byPhase[phase]) byPhase[phase] = [];
    757 +        byPhase[phase].push(card.id);
    758 +    });
    759 +
    760 +    const phaseIndex = {};
    761 +    for (const [phase, ids] of Object.entries(byPhase)) {
    762 +        ids.forEach((id, idx) => {
    763 +            phaseIndex[id] = { idx, count: ids.length };
    764 +        });
    765 +    }
    766 +
    767 +    cards.forEach((card) => {
    768 +        const rawPos = card.wheel_config && typeof card.wheel_config.position_degrees === 'number'
    769 +            ? card.wheel_config.position_degrees
    770 +            : null;
    771 +        if (rawPos != null) {
    772 +            card.wheel_config.position_degrees = normalizeDegrees(rawPos);
    773 +            return;
    774 +        }
    775 +
    776 +        const phase = card.wheel_config.phase;
    777 +        const ratio = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(PHASE_ANCHOR_RATIO, phase)
    778 +            ? PHASE_ANCHOR_RATIO[phase]
    779 +            : PHASE_ANCHOR_RATIO.action;
    780 +        const anchor = angleOnArc(arc, ratio);
    781 +        const placement = phaseIndex[card.id] || { idx: 0, count: 1 };
    782 +        const spread = arc.phase_spread_degrees;
    783 +        const localOffset = placement.count <= 1
    784 +            ? 0
    785 +            : ((placement.idx / (placement.count - 1)) - 0.5) * spread;
    786 +        const signedOffset = arc.direction === 'anticlockwise' ? -localOffset : localOffset;
    787 +        card.wheel_config.position_degrees = normalizeDegrees(anchor + signedOffset);
    788 +    });
    789 +}
    790 +
    791 +function normalizeAlgorithm(rawAlgorithm) {
    792 +    const raw = rawAlgorithm && typeof rawAlgorithm === 'object' ? rawAlgorithm : deepClone(DEFAULT_ALGORITHM);
    793 +    const meta = raw.algorithm_meta && typeof raw.algorithm_meta === 'object'
    794 +        ? deepClone(raw.algorithm_meta)
    795 +        : {};
    796 +    const arc = normalizeWheelArc(meta);
    797 +    const rawDeck = Array.isArray(raw.deck) ? raw.deck : [];
    798 +    const cards = rawDeck
    799 +        .filter((card) => card && typeof card === 'object' && card.id)
    800 +        .map((card) => ({
    801 +            ...deepClone(card),
    802 +            id: String(card.id),
    803 +            content: card.content && typeof card.content === 'object' ? deepClone(card.content) : { title: String(card.id), body: '' },
    804 +            checklist: Array.isArray(card.checklist) ? deepClone(card.checklist) : [],
    805 +            wheel_config: card.wheel_config && typeof card.wheel_config === 'object' ? deepClone(card.wheel_config) : {},
    806 +        }));
    807 +
    808 +    cards.forEach((card, idx) => {
    809 +        card.wheel_config.phase = inferPhase(card);
    810 +        card.transitions = normalizeTransitions(card, idx, cards);
    811 +    });
    812 +    derivePositionDegrees(cards, arc);
    813 +
    814 +    meta.wheel_arc = arc;
    815 +    return {
    816 +        algorithm_meta: meta,
    817 +        deck: cards,
    818 +    };
    819 +}
    820 +
    821 +function rebuildDeckMap(cards) {
    822 +    Object.keys(DECK).forEach((key) => {
    823 +        delete DECK[key];
    824 +    });
    825 +    cards.forEach((card) => {
    826 +        DECK[card.id] = card;
    827 +    });
    828 +}
    829 +
    830 +function currentAlgorithmStartId() {
    831 +    return RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.deck.length > 0 ? RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.deck[0].id : null;
    832 +}
    833 +
    834 +function syncHeaderFromAlgorithm() {
    835 +    const titleEl = document.querySelector('.app-title');
    836 +    if (!titleEl) return;
    837 +    const title = RUNTIME_ALGORITHM && RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.algorithm_meta
    838 +        ? RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.algorithm_meta.title
    839 +        : '';
    840 +    titleEl.textContent = title || 'Resuscitation Handbook';
    841 +}
    842 +
    843 +function applyAlgorithm(rawAlgorithm, sourceLabel) {
    844 +    RUNTIME_ALGORITHM = normalizeAlgorithm(rawAlgorithm);
    845 +    rebuildDeckMap(RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.deck);
    846 +    state.currentId = currentAlgorithmStartId();
    847 +    state.history = [];
    848 +    state.decisionIndex = 0;
    849 +    state.carouselIndex = 0;
    850 +    state.checklistState = {};
    851 +    state.wheel.dragOrigin = null;
    852 +    state.wheel.cardEntryAngles = {};
    853 +
    854 +    if (state.currentId && DECK[state.currentId]) {
    855 +        state.wheel.mode = computeWheelMode(DECK[state.currentId]);
    856 +        state.wheel.angle = canonicalAngleForCard(DECK[state.currentId], state.wheel.angle == null ? 330 : state.wheel.angle);
    857 +    } else {
    858 +        state.wheel.mode = 'LINEAR';
    859 +        state.wheel.angle = 330;
    860 +    }
    861 +
    862 +    resetTimerState();
    863 +    syncHeaderFromAlgorithm();
    864 +    render();
    865 +    return {
    866 +        ok: true,
    867 +        source: sourceLabel || 'inline-default',
    868 +        algorithm_id: RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.algorithm_meta && RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.algorithm_meta.id
    869 +            ? RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.algorithm_meta.id
    870 +            : null,
    871 +        card_count: RUNTIME_ALGORITHM.deck.length,
    872 +    };
    873 +}
    874 +
    875 +function sanitizeAlgorithmFile(value) {
    876 +    const text = String(value || '').trim();
    877 +    if (!text) return null;
    878 +    if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+\.json$/.test(text)) return null;
    879 +    return text;
    880 +}
    881 +
    882 +async function discoverAvailableAlgorithmFiles() {
    883 +    try {
    884 +        const res = await fetch('/algorithms/index.json', { cache: 'no-store' });
    885 +        if (!res.ok) return [...DEFAULT_ALGORITHM_FILES];
    886 +        const payload = await res.json();
    887 +        if (!payload || !Array.isArray(payload.algorithms)) return [...DEFAULT_ALGORITHM_FILES];
    888 +        const files = payload.algorithms
    889 +            .map((row) => sanitizeAlgorithmFile(row && row.file))
    890 +            .filter(Boolean);
    891 +        if (files.length === 0) return [...DEFAULT_ALGORITHM_FILES];
    892 +        return Array.from(new Set(files));
    893 +    } catch {
    894 +        return [...DEFAULT_ALGORITHM_FILES];
    895 +    }
    896 +}
    897 +
    898 +async function loadAlgorithmByFileName(fileName) {
    899 +    const safe = sanitizeAlgorithmFile(fileName);
    900 +    if (!safe) throw new Error(`Invalid algorithm file: ${fileName}`);
    901 +    const res = await fetch(`/algorithms/${safe}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
    902 +    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to load /algorithms/${safe} (HTTP ${res.status})`);
    903 +    return res.json();
    904 +}
    905 +
    906 +async function initAlgorithmFromUrl() {
    907 +    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    908 +    const requested = sanitizeAlgorithmFile(params.get('algo'));
    909 +    if (!requested) {
    910 +        applyAlgorithm(DEFAULT_ALGORITHM, 'inline-default');
    911 +        return;
    912 +    }
    913 +    try {
    914 +        const loaded = await loadAlgorithmByFileName(requested);
    915 +        applyAlgorithm(loaded, requested);
    916 +    } catch (err) {
    917 +        console.error(err);
    918 +        applyAlgorithm(DEFAULT_ALGORITHM, 'inline-default-fallback');
    919 +    }
    920 +}
    921 +
    922  // ========================================

─ Worked for 1m 01s ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

• Yes, this dual model is the right design.

  From what I studied in nonprofit/scripts, the pattern is already there:

  - Warden path: workspace-driven Blueprint run in .../nonprofit/scripts/blueprint_warden_run_nonprofit.js.
  - Traditional/system path: direct project scan in .../nonprofit/scripts/blueprint_run_current.js.
  - Unified orchestrator: .../nonprofit/scripts/run_unified_analysis.js.
  - Scan + parity + design bundle are separate stages (scan.js, check_dev_mode_parity.js, warden_design_bundle.js).

  For this repo, you already mirror most of that in:

  - scripts/blueprint_warden_run_resuscitation.js
  - scripts/blueprint_run_current.js
  - scripts/run_unified_analysis.js
  - scripts/scan.js
  - scripts/warden_design_bundle.js

  What to add for your exact dual workflow:

  1. Keep two execution lanes.

  - warden lane: assistant-operated, ticket/research/design outputs in appendix/guidance/warden/*.
  - system lane: your traditional run path, outputs in a separate system namespace (or keep current appendix/blueprint_outputs + appendix/scans_outputs and add system summary folder).

  2. Add input-source mode to both lanes.

  - --source local with --project-root app.
  - --source github with --repo owner/name --ref main --token-env GITHUB_TOKEN.
  - Blueprint already supports GitHub targets (type: github) in appendix/blueprint/src/loader/schema.py and appendix/blueprint/src/loader/github.py.

  3. Important behavior split.

  - Local source: full pipeline (parity + scan + design bundle).
  - GitHub source: Blueprint works immediately; scan/design should be optional unless --base-url is provided (because scan needs a running app endpoint).

  4. Keep artifacts traceable.

  - Every run manifest should include: mode (warden|system), source_type (local|github), source_ref (path or repo@ref), and output roots.

  My recommendation: implement a 2x2 command matrix next.

  - analyze:warden:local
  - analyze:warden:github
  - analyze:system:local
  - analyze:system:github

  This gives you exactly the separation you described: I operate in warden space, you operate in traditional space, both can pull from local or GitHub inputs with clean output boundaries.
  If you want, I can implement this matrix now in your scripts and package commands.

      7 +const { URL } = require('url');
      8 +const { spawn, spawnSync } = require('child_process');
      9 +
     10 +const ROOT = process.cwd();
     11 +const DEFAULT_BASE_URL = process.env.ANALYZE_BASE_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:3000';
     12 +const DEFAULT_PROJECT_ROOT = path.join(ROOT, 'app');
     13 +const DEFAULT_GITHUB_REF = 'main';
     14 +
     15 +const LANE_DEFAULTS = {
     16 +  warden: {
     17 +    blueprintOutputRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/guidance/warden/blueprint_outputs'),
     18 +    scanOutputRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/guidance/warden/scans_outputs'),
     19 +    designRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/guidance/warden/design'),
     20 +    workspacePath: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/blueprint/blueprint_outputs_warden'),
     21 +  },
     22 +  system: {
     23 +    blueprintOutputRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/system/blueprint_outputs'),
     24 +    scanOutputRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/system/scans_outputs'),
     25 +    designRoot: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/system/design'),
     26 +    workspacePath: path.join(ROOT, 'appendix/blueprint/blueprint_outputs_system'),
     27 +  },
     28 +};
     29 +
     30 +function rel(filePath) {
     31 +  return path.relative(ROOT, filePath) || '.';
     32 +}
     33 +
     34 +function latestDirectory(dirPath) {
     35 +  const fs = require('fs');
     36 +  if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) return null;
     37 +  const dirs = fs.readdirSync(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true })
     38 +    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())
     39 +    .map((entry) => path.join(dirPath, entry.name));
     40 +  if (dirs.length === 0) return null;
     41 +  dirs.sort((a, b) => fs.statSync(b).mtimeMs - fs.statSync(a).mtimeMs);
     42 +  return dirs[0];
     43 +}
     44 +
     45 +function parseArgs(argv) {
     46 +  const out = {
     47 +    lane: 'warden',
     48 +    source: 'local',
     49 +    baseUrl: DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
     50 +    keepDev: false,
     51 +    skipParity: false,
     52 +    skipBlueprint: false,
     53 +    skipScan: false,
     54 +    skipDesignBundle: false,
     55 +    includeUiStages: false,
     56 +    devTimeoutSeconds: 180,
     57 +    withCertificate: null,
     58 +
     59 +    projectRoot: DEFAULT_PROJECT_ROOT,
     60 +    repo: '',
     61 +    ref: DEFAULT_GITHUB_REF,
     62 +    tokenEnv: '',
     63 +    targetName: '',
     64 +    preset: 'standard',
     65 +
     66 +    blueprintOutputRoot: '',
     67 +    scanOutputRoot: '',
     68 +    designRoot: '',
     69 +    workspacePath: '',
     70 +  };
     71 +
     72 +  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
     73 +    const arg = argv[i];
     74 +    if (arg === '--lane') {
     75 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
     76 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --lane');
     77 +      out.lane = next.trim().toLowerCase();
     78 +      i += 1;
     79 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--lane=')) {
     80 +      out.lane = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim().toLowerCase();
     81 +    } else if (arg === '--source') {
     82 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
     83 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --source');
     84 +      out.source = next.trim().toLowerCase();
     85 +      i += 1;
     86 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--source=')) {
     87 +      out.source = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim().toLowerCase();
     88 +    } else if (arg === '--project-root') {
     89 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
     90 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --project-root');
     91 +      out.projectRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, next);
     92 +      i += 1;
     93 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--project-root=')) {
     94 +      out.projectRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
     95 +    } else if (arg === '--repo') {
     96 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
     97 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --repo');
     98 +      out.repo = next.trim();
     99 +      i += 1;
    100 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--repo=')) {
    101 +      out.repo = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim();
    102 +    } else if (arg === '--ref') {
    103 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    104 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --ref');
    105 +      out.ref = next.trim();
    106 +      i += 1;
    107 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--ref=')) {
    108 +      out.ref = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim();
    109 +    } else if (arg === '--token-env') {
    110 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    111 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --token-env');
    112 +      out.tokenEnv = next.trim();
    113 +      i += 1;
    114 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--token-env=')) {
    115 +      out.tokenEnv = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim();
    116 +    } else if (arg === '--target-name') {
    117 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    118 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --target-name');
    119 +      out.targetName = next.trim();
    120 +      i += 1;
    121 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--target-name=')) {
    122 +      out.targetName = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim();
    123 +    } else if (arg === '--preset') {
    124 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    125 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --preset');
    126 +      out.preset = next.trim();
    127 +      i += 1;
    128 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--preset=')) {
    129 +      out.preset = arg.split('=', 2)[1].trim();
    130 +    } else if (arg === '--base-url') {
    131 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    132 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --base-url');
    133 +      out.baseUrl = next;
    134 +      i += 1;
    135 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--base-url=')) {
    136 +      out.baseUrl = arg.split('=', 2)[1];
    137 +    } else if (arg === '--blueprint-output-root') {
    138 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    139 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --blueprint-output-root');
    140 +      out.blueprintOutputRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, next);
    141 +      i += 1;
    142 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--blueprint-output-root=')) {
    143 +      out.blueprintOutputRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
    144 +    } else if (arg === '--scan-output-root') {
    145 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    146 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --scan-output-root');
    147 +      out.scanOutputRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, next);
    148 +      i += 1;
    149 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--scan-output-root=')) {
    150 +      out.scanOutputRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
    151 +    } else if (arg === '--design-root') {
    152 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    153 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --design-root');
    154 +      out.designRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, next);
    155 +      i += 1;
    156 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--design-root=')) {
    157 +      out.designRoot = path.resolve(ROOT, arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
    158 +    } else if (arg === '--workspace-path') {
    159 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    160 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --workspace-path');
    161 +      out.workspacePath = path.resolve(ROOT, next);
    162 +      i += 1;
    163 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--workspace-path=')) {
    164 +      out.workspacePath = path.resolve(ROOT, arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
    165 +    } else if (arg === '--keep-dev') {
    166 +      out.keepDev = true;
    167 +    } else if (arg === '--skip-parity') {
    168 +      out.skipParity = true;
    169 +    } else if (arg === '--skip-blueprint') {
    170 +      out.skipBlueprint = true;
    171 +    } else if (arg === '--skip-scan') {
    172 +      out.skipScan = true;
    173 +    } else if (arg === '--skip-design-bundle') {
    174 +      out.skipDesignBundle = true;
    175 +    } else if (arg === '--include-ui-stages') {
    176 +      out.includeUiStages = true;
    177 +    } else if (arg === '--with-certificate') {
    178 +      out.withCertificate = true;
    179 +    } else if (arg === '--no-certificate') {
    180 +      out.withCertificate = false;
    181 +    } else if (arg === '--dev-timeout') {
    182 +      const next = argv[i + 1];
    183 +      if (!next || next.startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value after --dev-timeout');
    184 +      out.devTimeoutSeconds = Number(next);
    185 +      i += 1;
    186 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--dev-timeout=')) {
    187 +      out.devTimeoutSeconds = Number(arg.split('=', 2)[1]);
    188 +    } else if (arg === '--help' || arg === '-h') {
    189 +      console.log(`
    190 +Usage:
    191 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js
    192 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane warden --source local
    193 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane system --source local
    194 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane warden --source github --repo owner/repo --ref main
    195 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane system --source github --repo owner/repo --ref main
    196 +
    197 +Default pipeline:
    198 +  1) Optional parity check (local source only)
    199 +  2) Blueprint workspace run (lane-aware outputs)
    200 +  3) Optional UI stages: ensure local dev server, then scan + design bundle
    201 +  4) Stop dev server if this script started it (unless --keep-dev)
    202 +
    203 +Dual-mode examples:
    204 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane warden --source local
    205 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane system --source local
    206 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane warden --source github --repo projecthamburgresearch/resuscitation_health_app
    207 +  node scripts/run_unified_analysis.js --lane system --source github --repo projecthamburgresearch/resuscitation_health_app --include-ui-stages
    208 +
    209 +Flags:
    210 +  --lane warden|system        Output/operation lane (default: warden)
    211 +  --source local|github       Blueprint input source (default: local)
    212 +  --project-root PATH         Local project root for Blueprint (default: app)
    213 +  --repo owner/name           GitHub repo when --source github
    214 +  --ref REF                   GitHub branch/tag/commit (default: main)
    215 +  --token-env ENV             Env var holding GitHub token for private repos
    216 +  --target-name NAME          Explicit Blueprint target name
    217 +  --preset quick|standard|deep
    218 +  --with-certificate          Enable Blueprint certificate check
    219 +  --no-certificate            Disable Blueprint certificate check
    220 +  --include-ui-stages         Allow scan + design for github source
    221 +  --base-url URL              UI base URL for scan/design (default: ${DEFAULT_BASE_URL})
    222 +  --keep-dev                  Keep dev server running if started by this script
    223 +  --skip-parity               Skip parity stage
    224 +  --skip-blueprint            Skip blueprint stage
    225 +  --skip-scan                 Skip scan stage
    226 +  --skip-design-bundle        Skip design bundle stage
    227 +  --dev-timeout SEC           Dev server readiness timeout (default: 180)
    228 +
    229 +Output root overrides:
    230 +  --blueprint-output-root PATH
    231 +  --scan-output-root PATH
    232 +  --design-root PATH
    233 +  --workspace-path PATH
    234 +`);
    235 +      process.exit(0);
    236 +    } else if (arg.startsWith('--')) {
    237 +      throw new Error(`Unknown flag: ${arg}`);
    238 +    }
    239 +  }
    240 +
    241 +  if (!['warden', 'system'].includes(out.lane)) {
    242 +    throw new Error(`Invalid --lane '${out.lane}'. Use warden or system.`);
    243 +  }
    244 +  if (!['local', 'github'].includes(out.source)) {
    245 +    throw new Error(`Invalid --source '${out.source}'. Use local or github.`);
    246 +  }
    247 +  if (out.source === 'github' && (!out.repo || !out.repo.includes('/'))) {
    248 +    throw new Error(`GitHub source requires --repo owner/name. Received: '${out.repo || ''}'`);
    249 +  }
    250 +  if (!['quick', 'standard', 'deep'].includes(out.preset)) {
    251 +    throw new Error(`Invalid --preset '${out.preset}'. Use quick, standard, or deep.`);
    252 +  }
    253 +
    254 +  if (!Number.isFinite(out.devTimeoutSeconds) || out.devTimeoutSeconds <= 0) {
    255 +    throw new Error('Invalid --dev-timeout value. Must be a positive number.');
    256 +  }
    257 +
    258 +  const laneDefaults = LANE_DEFAULTS[out.lane];
    259 +  out.blueprintOutputRoot = out.blueprintOutputRoot || laneDefaults.blueprintOutputRoot;
    260 +  out.scanOutputRoot = out.scanOutputRoot || laneDefaults.scanOutputRoot;
    261 +  out.designRoot = out.designRoot || laneDefaults.designRoot;
    262 +  out.workspacePath = out.workspacePath || laneDefaults.workspacePath;
    263 +  if (out.withCertificate === null) {
    264 +    out.withCertificate = out.lane === 'warden';
    265 +  }
    266 +  if (!out.targetName) {
    267 +    out.targetName = out.source === 'github'
    268 +      ? out.repo.split('/').pop()
    269 +      : `resuscitation-app-${out.lane}`;
    270 +  }
    271 +
    272 +  return out;
    273 +}
    274 +
    275 +function runStep(label, cmd, args, options = {}) {
    276 +  console.log(`\n[Step] ${label}`);
    277 +  console.log(`  $ ${[cmd, ...args].join(' ')}`);
    278 +  const result = spawnSync(cmd, args, {
    279 +    cwd: ROOT,
    280 +    stdio: 'inherit',
    281 +    env: { ...process.env, ...(options.env || {}) },
    282 +  });
    283 +  if (result.status !== 0) {
    284 +    throw new Error(`${label} failed with exit code ${result.status || 1}`);
    285 +  }
    286 +}
    287 +
    288 +function sleep(ms) {
    289 +  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
    290 +}
    291 +
    292 +function requestUrl(urlStr, timeoutMs = 3000) {
    293 +  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    294 +    const parsed = new URL(urlStr);
    295 +    const lib = parsed.protocol === 'https:' ? https : http;
    296 +    const req = lib.request(
    297 +      parsed,
    298 +      { method: 'GET', timeout: timeoutMs },
    299 +      (res) => {
    300 +        const status = Number(res.statusCode || 0);
    301 +        res.resume();
    302 +        resolve({ ok: status >= 200 && status < 400, status });
    303 +      },
    304 +    );
    305 +    req.on('timeout', () => {
    306 +      req.destroy(new Error('timeout'));
    307 +    });
    308 +    req.on('error', () => resolve({ ok: false, status: 0 }));
    309 +    req.end();
    310 +  });
    311 +}
    312 +
    313 +async function isDevReady(baseUrl) {
    314 +  const checks = [
    315 +    `${baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/`,
    316 +    `${baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/dev-mode`,
    317 +  ];
    318 +  for (const url of checks) {
    319 +    const res = await requestUrl(url);
    320 +    if (!res.ok) return false;
    321 +  }
    322 +  return true;
    323 +}
    324 +
    325 +function parsePort(baseUrl) {
    326 +  const parsed = new URL(baseUrl);
    327 +  if (parsed.port) return Number(parsed.port);
    328 +  return parsed.protocol === 'https:' ? 443 : 80;
    329 +}
    330 +
    331 +function waitForChildExit(child, timeoutMs) {
    332 +  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    333 +    let done = false;
    334 +    const finish = () => {
    335 +      if (done) return;
    336 +      done = true;
    337 +      resolve();
    338 +    };
    339 +    child.once('exit', finish);
    340 +    setTimeout(finish, timeoutMs);
    341 +  });
    342 +}
    343 +
    344 +async function stopDevServer(child) {
    345 +  if (!child || child.killed) return;
    346 +  child.kill('SIGTERM');
    347 +  await waitForChildExit(child, 8000);
    348 +  if (!child.killed) {
    349 +    try {
    350 +      child.kill('SIGKILL');
    351 +    } catch {
    352 +      // best effort
    353 +    }
    354 +  }
    355 +}
    356 +
    357 +async function ensureDevServer(baseUrl, timeoutSeconds) {
    358 +  const readyAlready = await isDevReady(baseUrl);
    359 +  if (readyAlready) {
    360 +    console.log('\n[Info] Dev server already available; reusing existing process.');
    361 +    return { started: false, child: null };
    362 +  }
    363 +
    364 +  const port = parsePort(baseUrl);
    365 +  console.log('\n[Step] Start Dev Server');
    366 +  console.log(`  Starting static dev server on port ${port}...`);
    367 +  const child = spawn('npm', ['run', 'dev', '--', '--port', String(port)], {
    368 +    cwd: ROOT,
    369 +    stdio: 'inherit',
    370 +    env: { ...process.env, PORT: String(port) },
    371 +  });
    372 +
    373 +  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutSeconds * 1000;
    374 +  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    375 +    if (child.exitCode !== null) {
    376 +      throw new Error(`Dev server exited early with code ${child.exitCode}`);
    377 +    }
    378 +    const ready = await isDevReady(baseUrl);
    379 +    if (ready) {
    380 +      console.log('[Info] Dev server is ready.');
    381 +      return { started: true, child };
    382 +    }
    383 +    await sleep(1000);
    384 +  }
    385 +
    386 +  await stopDevServer(child);
    387 +  throw new Error(`Dev server not ready within ${timeoutSeconds}s`);
    388 +}
    389 +
    390 +function latestRunFromOutputRoot(outputRoot, label) {
    391 +  const runsDir = path.join(outputRoot, 'runs');
    392 +  const latest = latestDirectory(runsDir);
    393 +  if (!latest) {
    394 +    throw new Error(`No ${label} runs found under ${rel(runsDir)}`);
    395 +  }
    396 +  return latest;
    397 +}
    398 +
    399 +function buildBlueprintArgs(opts) {
    400 +  const args = [
    401 +    'scripts/blueprint_warden_run_resuscitation.js',
    402 +    '--lane', opts.lane,
    403 +    '--source', opts.source,
    404 +    '--output-root', opts.blueprintOutputRoot,
    405 +    '--workspace-path', opts.workspacePath,
    406 +    '--target-name', opts.targetName,
    407 +    '--preset', opts.preset,
    408 +  ];
    409 +
    410 +  if (opts.source === 'github') {
    411 +    args.push('--repo', opts.repo);
    412 +    args.push('--ref', opts.ref || DEFAULT_GITHUB_REF);
    413 +    if (opts.tokenEnv) args.push('--token-env', opts.tokenEnv);
    414 +  } else {
    415 +    args.push('--project-root', opts.projectRoot);
    416 +  }
    417 +
    418 +  if (!opts.withCertificate) args.push('--no-certificate');
    419 +
    420 +  return args;
    421 +}
    422 +
    423 +async function main() {
    424 +  const opts = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
    425 +  const allowUiStages = opts.source === 'local' || opts.includeUiStages;
    426 +  const shouldRunParity = !opts.skipParity && opts.source === 'local';
    427 +  const shouldRunScan = !opts.skipScan && allowUiStages;
    428 +  const shouldRunDesign = !opts.skipDesignBundle && allowUiStages;
    429 +
    430 +  if (!allowUiStages && (!opts.skipScan || !opts.skipDesignBundle || !opts.skipParity)) {
    431 +    console.log('\n[Info] GitHub source defaults to Blueprint-only operation.');
    432 +    console.log('       Add --include-ui-stages to run parity/scan/design against a UI base URL.');
    433 +  }
    434 +
    435 +  console.log('\nUnified Analysis Pipeline');
    436 +  console.log(`  Lane          : ${opts.lane}`);
    437 +  console.log(`  Source        : ${opts.source}`);
    438 +  if (opts.source === 'github') {
    439 +    console.log(`  Repo          : ${opts.repo}`);
    440 +    console.log(`  Ref           : ${opts.ref}`);
    441 +    if (opts.tokenEnv) console.log(`  Token env     : ${opts.tokenEnv}`);
    442 +  } else {
    443 +    console.log(`  Project root  : ${rel(opts.projectRoot)}`);
    444 +  }
    445 +  console.log(`  Base URL      : ${opts.baseUrl}`);
    446 +  console.log(`  Keep Dev      : ${opts.keepDev}`);
    447 +  console.log(`  Run Parity    : ${shouldRunParity}`);
    448 +  console.log(`  Run Blueprint : ${!opts.skipBlueprint}`);
    449 +  console.log(`  Run Scan      : ${shouldRunScan}`);
    450 +  console.log(`  Run Design    : ${shouldRunDesign}`);
    451 +  console.log(`  Certificate   : ${opts.withCertificate ? 'enabled' : 'skipped'}`);
    452 +  console.log(`  Blueprint out : ${rel(opts.blueprintOutputRoot)}`);
    453 +  console.log(`  Scan out      : ${rel(opts.scanOutputRoot)}`);
    454 +  console.log(`  Design out    : ${rel(opts.designRoot)}`);
    455 +  console.log(`  Workspace     : ${rel(opts.workspacePath)}`);
    456 +
    457 +  let devHandle = { started: false, child: null };
    458 +  try {
    459 +    if (shouldRunParity) {
    460 +      runStep('Check Dev-Mode Parity', 'node', ['scripts/check_dev_mode_parity.js']);
    461 +    }
    462 +
    463 +    if (!opts.skipBlueprint) {
    464 +      runStep('Blueprint Workspace Run', 'node', buildBlueprintArgs(opts));
    465 +    }
    466 +
    467 +    const needsDevServer = shouldRunScan || shouldRunDesign;
    468 +    if (needsDevServer) {
    469 +      devHandle = await ensureDevServer(opts.baseUrl, opts.devTimeoutSeconds);
    470 +    }
    471 +
    472 +    if (shouldRunScan) {
    473 +      runStep('Scan Coverage', 'node', ['scripts/scan.js', '--all', '--diff', '--coverage-report'], {
    474 +        env: {
    475 +          SCAN_BASE_URL: opts.baseUrl,
    476 +          SCAN_OUTPUT_DIR: opts.scanOutputRoot,
    477 +        },
    478 +      });
    479 +    }
    480 +
    481 +    if (shouldRunDesign) {
    482 +      const blueprintRun = latestRunFromOutputRoot(opts.blueprintOutputRoot, 'Blueprint');
    483 +      const scanRun = latestRunFromOutputRoot(opts.scanOutputRoot, 'Scan');
    484 +      runStep('Design Bundle', 'node', [
    485 +        'scripts/warden_design_bundle.js',
    486 +        '--mode',
    487 +        'main',
    488 +        '--base-url',
    489 +        opts.baseUrl,
    490 +        '--blueprint-run',
    491 +        blueprintRun,
    492 +        '--scan-run',
    493 +        scanRun,
    494 +      ], {
    495 +        env: {
    496 +          WARDEN_DESIGN_ROOT: opts.designRoot,
    497 +          WARDEN_BLUEPRINT_RUNS_DIR: path.join(opts.blueprintOutputRoot, 'runs'),
    498 +          WARDEN_SCAN_RUNS_DIR: path.join(opts.scanOutputRoot, 'runs'),
    499 +        },
    500 +      });
    501 +    }
    502 +  } finally {
    503 +    if (devHandle.started && !opts.keepDev) {
    504 +      console.log('\n[Step] Stop Dev Server');
    505 +      await stopDevServer(devHandle.child);
    506 +      console.log('[Info] Dev server stopped.');
    507 +    } else if (devHandle.started && opts.keepDev) {
    508 +      console.log('\n[Info] Keeping dev server running (--keep-dev).');
    509 +    }
    510 +  }
    511 +
    512 +  console.log('\nPipeline complete.');
    513 +  console.log('Outputs:');
    514 +  console.log(`  - Blueprint current: ${rel(path.join(opts.blueprintOutputRoot, 'current'))}/`);

• Added docs/DUAL_RUN_MODES.md (+47 -0)
     1 +# Dual Run Modes
     2 +
     3 +This project now supports a dual execution model:
     4 +
     5 +- `warden` lane: assistant-operated workflow space
     6 +- `system` lane: traditional project workflow space
     7 +
     8 +Each lane supports two Blueprint input sources:
     9 +
    10 +- `local` (analyze local `app` files)
    11 +- `github` (analyze a GitHub repo via Blueprint workspace target)
    12 +
    13 +## Matrix Commands
    14 +
    15 +Use these `npm` commands:
    16 +
    17 +- `npm run analyze:warden:local`
    18 +- `npm run analyze:warden:github -- --repo owner/repo`
    19 +- `npm run analyze:system:local`
    20 +- `npm run analyze:system:github -- --repo owner/repo`
    21 +
    22 +Optional GitHub flags:
    23 +
    24 +- `--ref main`
    25 +- `--token-env GITHUB_TOKEN` (for private repos)
    26 +
    27 +## Output Spaces
    28 +
    29 +Warden lane outputs:
    30 +
    31 +- Blueprint: `appendix/guidance/warden/blueprint_outputs`
    32 +- Scan: `appendix/guidance/warden/scans_outputs`
    33 +- Design bundle: `appendix/guidance/warden/design`
    34 +
    35 +System lane outputs:
    36 +
    37 +- Blueprint: `appendix/system/blueprint_outputs`
    38 +- Scan: `appendix/system/scans_outputs`
    39 +- Design bundle: `appendix/system/design`
    40 +
    41 +## Notes
    42 +
    43 +- For `--source github`, the pipeline defaults to Blueprint-only.
    44 +- Add `--include-ui-stages` if you also want scan/design against a running UI base URL.
    45 +- Certificate defaults:
    46 +  - `warden` lane: enabled
    47 +  - `system` lane: skipped

• I found and fixed an important blocker: Blueprint’s current workspace runner fails on native type: github targets before clone. I’m patching our wrapper to pre-sync the GitHub repo into workspace intake/
  local and then run it as a local target, so GitHub source mode is reliable.

• Edited scripts/blueprint_warden_run_resuscitation.js (+56 -10)
    268
    269 +function resolveWorkspaceGithubCloneDir(opts) {
    270 +  const repoName = (opts.repo || '').split('/').pop() || 'repo';
    271 +  const folder = opts.targetName || repoName;
    272 +  return path.join(opts.workspacePath, 'intake', 'local', folder);
    273 +}
    274 +
    275 +function buildGithubCloneUrl(opts) {
    276 +  if (opts.tokenEnv) {
    277 +    const token = process.env[opts.tokenEnv] || '';
    278 +    if (!token) {
    279 +      throw new Error(`Missing GitHub token env '${opts.tokenEnv}' for private repo access.`);
    280 +    }
    281 +    return `https://${token}@github.com/${opts.repo}.git`;
    282 +  }
    283 +  return `https://github.com/${opts.repo}.git`;
    284 +}
    285 +
    286 +function ensureWorkspaceGithubClone(opts) {
    287 +  const cloneDir = resolveWorkspaceGithubCloneDir(opts);
    288 +  const gitDir = path.join(cloneDir, '.git');
    289 +  const ref = opts.ref || DEFAULT_GITHUB_REF;
    290 +  const cloneUrl = buildGithubCloneUrl(opts);
    291 +
    292 +  if (fs.existsSync(cloneDir) && !fs.existsSync(gitDir)) {
    293 +    throw new Error(`Workspace clone dir exists but is not a git repo: ${rel(cloneDir)}`);
    294 +  }
    295 +
    296 +  if (fs.existsSync(gitDir)) {
    297 +    runCommand('git', ['-C', cloneDir, 'fetch', '--all'], ROOT, 'Git fetch');
    298 +    runCommand('git', ['-C', cloneDir, 'checkout', ref], ROOT, 'Git checkout');
    299 +    runCommand('git', ['-C', cloneDir, 'pull', '--ff-only'], ROOT, 'Git pull');
    300 +    return cloneDir;
    301 +  }
    302 +
    303 +  ensureDir(path.dirname(cloneDir));
    304 +  runCommand('git', ['clone', '--branch', ref, '--depth', '1', cloneUrl, cloneDir], ROOT, 'Git clone');
    305 +  return cloneDir;
    306 +}
